QUESTION TEXT AND CODE CATEGORIES
Before you became pregnant with (your (Nth) pregnancy
which ended in (Computed variable PREGEND)/(BABY
NAME)), which of the methods on Card E-6 was the last
method of birth control that you were using? If you
were using two or more methods at the same time, please
tell me about this. (For example, a woman might use
the pill and the man might use a condom on the same
occasion.) (4th MENTIONED)
Inapplicable: R has never had voluntary sex (CH-8
EVRHDVOL NE 1); R first had sex after 1/91 (CH-1
WNFSTSEX GE 1093); R is currently pregnant for the
first time (BB-1 NUMPREGS = 1 and CURRPREG = 1); R's
pregnancy interval began after 1/91; or R did not use a
birth control method in the interval (coded 2 in ED-1
EVUSEINT); R's pregnancy began after 1/91 (computed
variable CONCEPT GE 1093); R's pregnancy began on or
after 1/91 and the date she stopped using a birth
control method was on or after 1/91 (computed variable
CONCEPT GE 1093 and ED-3 DATESTOP GE 1093); or R
mentioned fewer than 4 methods used prior to this
pregnancy.

Note: See section on "data quality" for discussion

EVRHDVOL NE 1); R's pregnancy interval began prior to
1/91, R has used a birth control method, but not in the
interval (beginning of interval was before 1/91,
computed variable EVERUSED = 1, and ED-1 EVUSEINT = 2);

her pregnancy began (beginning of interval was after

CONCEPT); or R had stopped using all methods of birth
control before she became pregnant (EG-1 STOPDUSE = 1);
or R mentioned fewer than 5 methods used around the
time of conception.
